### [v2.AnalyticsServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.analyticsserviceclient)

Service for managing & accessing retail search business metric. Retail recommendation business metric is currently not available. v2

### [v2.CatalogServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.catalogserviceclient)

Service for managing catalog configuration. v2

### [v2.CompletionServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.completionserviceclient)

Autocomplete service for retail.

This feature is only available for users who have Retail Search enabled. Enable Retail Search on Cloud Console before using this feature. v2

### [v2.ControlServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.controlserviceclient)

Service for modifying Control. v2

### [v2.ConversationalSearchServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.conversationalsearchserviceclient)

Service for retail conversational search.

This feature is only available for users who have Retail Conversational Search enabled. Enable Retail Conversational Search on Cloud Console before using this feature. v2

### [v2.GenerativeQuestionServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.generativequestionserviceclient)

Service for managing LLM generated questions in search serving. v2

### [v2.ModelServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.modelserviceclient)

Service for performing CRUD operations on models. Recommendation models contain all the metadata necessary to generate a set of models for the `Predict()` API. A model is queried indirectly via a ServingConfig, which associates a model with a given Placement (e.g. Frequently Bought Together on Home Page).

This service allows you to do the following:

* Initiate training of a model. * Pause training of an existing model. * List all the available models along with their metadata. * Control their tuning schedule. v2

### [v2.PredictionServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.predictionserviceclient)

Service for making recommendation prediction. v2

### [v2.ProductServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.productserviceclient)

Service for ingesting information of the customer's website. v2

### [v2.SearchServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.searchserviceclient)

Service for search.

This feature is only available for users who have Retail Search enabled. Enable Retail Search on Cloud Console before using this feature. v2

### [v2.ServingConfigServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.servingconfigserviceclient)

Service for modifying ServingConfig. v2

### [v2.UserEventServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2.usereventserviceclient)

Service for ingesting end user actions on the customer website. v2

es are automatically created when a [Catalog](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/protos.google.cloud.retail.v2alpha.catalog) is created. There are fixed three branches in each catalog, and may use method to get the details of all branches. v2alpha

Service for ingesting information of the customer's website. v2beta

### [v2beta.ProjectServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2beta.projectserviceclient)

Service for settings at Project level. v2beta

### [v2beta.SearchServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2beta.searchserviceclient)

Service for search.

This feature is only available for users who have Retail Search enabled. Enable Retail Search on Cloud Console before using this feature. v2beta

### [v2beta.ServingConfigServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2beta.servingconfigserviceclient)

Service for modifying ServingConfig. v2beta

### [v2beta.UserEventServiceClient](https://docs.cloud.google.com/nodejs/docs/reference/retail/latest/retail/v2beta.usereventserviceclient)

Service for ingesting end user actions on the customer website. v2beta
